Table 1 (pone-0084882-t001)

Caption: Subjective evaluation of the stimuli as a function of group.

Footnote: Y-Axis, the stimuli presented on a visual analogue scale (VAS) off-magnet, X-Axis, emotions elicited on 3 dimensions (i.e., disgust, fear, pleasure) for the three groups (i.e., vaginismus, dyspareunia and healthy controls). DIS, core disgust elicitors, A-R, animal-reminder disgust elicitors; FEA, fear related stimuli; PEN, explicit sexual penetration stimuli; BOD, neutral bodies; NEU, neutral objects. The VAS had a scale of 0 to 100, with high score indicating higher affect (pleasure/disgust/fear).

Caption: Shared brain responses between PEN-and aversive- brain activity maps.
